    64	// GetAgentHooks installs yyork's Codex hooks into the worktree-local
    65	// .codex/hooks.json file. Existing hook entries are preserved and duplicate
    66	// yyork commands are not appended.
    67	func (p *Plugin) GetAgentHooks(ctx context.Context, cfg agent.WorkspaceHookConfig) error {
    68		if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
    69			return err
    70		}
    71		if strings.TrimSpace(cfg.WorkspacePath) == "" {
    72			return errors.New("codex.GetAgentHooks: WorkspacePath is required")
    73		}
    74	
    75		hooksPath := codexHooksPath(cfg.WorkspacePath)
    76		topLevel, rawHooks, err := readCodexHooks(hooksPath)
    77		if err != nil {
    78			return fmt.Errorf("codex.GetAgentHooks: %w", err)
    79		}
    80	
    81		for event, specs := range groupCodexHooksByEvent() {
    82			var existingGroups []codexMatcherGroup
    83			if err := parseCodexHookType(rawHooks, event, &existingGroups); err != nil {
    84				return fmt.Errorf("codex.GetAgentHooks: %w", err)
    85			}
    86			for _, spec := range specs {
    87				if !codexHookCommandExists(existingGroups, spec.Command) {
    88					entry := codexHookEntry{Type: "command", Command: spec.Command, Timeout: codexHookTimeout}
    89					existingGroups = addCodexHook(existingGroups, entry)
    90				}
    91			}
    92			if err := marshalCodexHookType(rawHooks, event, existingGroups); err != nil {
    93				return fmt.Errorf("codex.GetAgentHooks: %w", err)
    94			}
    95		}
    96	
    97		if err := writeCodexHooks(hooksPath, topLevel, rawHooks); err != nil {
    98			return fmt.Errorf("codex.GetAgentHooks: %w", err)
    99		}
   100	
   101		if err := ensureCodexHooksFeatureEnabled(cfg.WorkspacePath); err != nil {
   102			return fmt.Errorf("codex.GetAgentHooks: enable hooks feature: %w", err)
   103		}
   104		return nil
   105	}
   106	
   107	// UninstallHooks removes yyork's Codex hooks from the workspace-local
   108	// .codex/hooks.json file, leaving user-defined hooks untouched. A missing file
   109	// is a no-op. The .codex/config.toml `hooks = true` feature flag is left in
   110	// place because it enables every Codex hook, not just yyork's.
   111	func (p *Plugin) UninstallHooks(ctx context.Context, workspacePath string) error {
   112		if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
   113			return err
   114		}
   115		if strings.TrimSpace(workspacePath) == "" {
   116			return errors.New("codex.UninstallHooks: workspacePath is required")
   117		}
   118	
   119		hooksPath := codexHooksPath(workspacePath)
   120		if _, err := os.Stat(hooksPath); errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
   121			return nil
   122		}
   123		topLevel, rawHooks, err := readCodexHooks(hooksPath)
   124		if err != nil {
   125			return fmt.Errorf("codex.UninstallHooks: %w", err)
   126		}
   127	
   128		for _, event := range codexManagedEvents() {
   129			var groups []codexMatcherGroup
   130			if err := parseCodexHookType(rawHooks, event, &groups); err != nil {
   131				return fmt.Errorf("codex.UninstallHooks: %w", err)
   132			}
   133			groups = removeCodexManagedHooks(groups)
   134			if err := marshalCodexHookType(rawHooks, event, groups); err != nil {
   135				return fmt.Errorf("codex.UninstallHooks: %w", err)
   136			}
   137		}
   138	
   139		if err := writeCodexHooks(hooksPath, topLevel, rawHooks); err != nil {
   140			return fmt.Errorf("codex.UninstallHooks: %w", err)
   141		}
   142		return nil
   143	}
   144	
   145	// AreHooksInstalled reports whether any yyork Codex hook is present in the
   146	// workspace-local hooks file. A missing file means none are installed.
   147	func (p *Plugin) AreHooksInstalled(ctx context.Context, workspacePath string) (bool, error) {
   148		if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
   149			return false, err
   150		}
   151		if strings.TrimSpace(workspacePath) == "" {
   152			return false, errors.New("codex.AreHooksInstalled: workspacePath is required")
   153		}
   154	
   155		hooksPath := codexHooksPath(workspacePath)
   156		if _, err := os.Stat(hooksPath); errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
   157			return false, nil
   158		}
   159		_, rawHooks, err := readCodexHooks(hooksPath)
   160		if err != nil {
   161			return false, fmt.Errorf("codex.AreHooksInstalled: %w", err)
   162		}
   163	
   164		for _, event := range codexManagedEvents() {
   165			var groups []codexMatcherGroup
   166			if err := parseCodexHookType(rawHooks, event, &groups); err != nil {
   167				return false, fmt.Errorf("codex.AreHooksInstalled: %w", err)
   168			}

   174				}
   175			}
   176		}
   177		return false, nil
   178	}
   179	
   180	func codexHooksPath(workspacePath string) string {
   181		return filepath.Join(workspacePath, codexHooksDirName, codexHooksFileName)
   182	}
   183	
   184	// readCodexHooks loads the hooks file into a top-level raw map plus the decoded
   185	// "hooks" sub-map, preserving keys yyork doesn't manage. A missing or empty
   186	// file yields empty maps.
   187	func readCodexHooks(hooksPath string) (map[string]json.RawMessage, map[string]json.RawMessage, error) {
   188		topLevel := map[string]json.RawMessage{}
   189		rawHooks := map[string]json.RawMessage{}
   190	
   191		data, err := os.ReadFile(hooksPath)
   192		if errors.Is(err, os.ErrNotExist) {
   193			return topLevel, rawHooks, nil
   194		}
   195		if err != nil {
   196			return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("read %s: %w", hooksPath, err)
   197		}
   198		if len(strings.TrimSpace(string(data))) == 0 {
   199			return topLevel, rawHooks, nil
   200		}
   201		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &topLevel); err != nil {
   202			return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("parse %s: %w", hooksPath, err)
   203		}
   204		if hooksRaw, ok := topLevel["hooks"]; ok {
   205			if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks); err != nil {
   206				return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("parse hooks in %s: %w", hooksPath, err)
   207			}
   208		}
   209		return topLevel, rawHooks, nil
   210	}
   211	
   212	// writeCodexHooks folds rawHooks back into topLevel and writes the file. An
   213	// empty hooks map drops the "hooks" key entirely.
   214	func writeCodexHooks(hooksPath string, topLevel, rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage) error {
   215		if len(rawHooks) == 0 {
   216			delete(topLevel, "hooks")
   217		} else {
   218			hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawHooks)
   219			if err != nil {
   220				return fmt.Errorf("encode hooks: %w", err)
   221			}
   222			topLevel["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   223		}
   224	
   225		if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(hooksPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   226			return fmt.Errorf("create hook dir: %w", err)
   227		}
   228		data, err := json.MarshalIndent(topLevel, "", "  ")
   229		if err != nil {
   230			return fmt.Errorf("encode %s: %w", hooksPath, err)
   231		}
   232		data = append(data, '\n')
   233		if err := os.WriteFile(hooksPath, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   234			return fmt.Errorf("write %s: %w", hooksPath, err)
   235		}
   236		return nil
   237	}
   238	
   239	// groupCodexHooksByEvent groups the managed hook specs by their Codex event so
   240	// each event's array is rewritten once.
   241	func groupCodexHooksByEvent() map[string][]codexHookSpec {
   242		byEvent := map[string][]codexHookSpec{}
   243		for _, spec := range codexManagedHooks {
   244			byEvent[spec.Event] = append(byEvent[spec.Event], spec)
   245		}
   246		return byEvent
   247	}
   248	
   249	// codexManagedEvents returns the distinct Codex events yyork manages, in the
   250	// order they first appear in codexManagedHooks.
   251	func codexManagedEvents() []string {
   252		seen := map[string]bool{}
   253		events := make([]string, 0, len(codexManagedHooks))
   254		for _, spec := range codexManagedHooks {
   255			if !seen[spec.Event] {
   256				seen[spec.Event] = true
   257				events = append(events, spec.Event)
   258			}
   259		}
   260		return events
   261	}
   262	
   263	func isCodexManagedHook(command string) bool {
   264		return strings.HasPrefix(command, codexLegacyHookCommandPrefix) ||
   265			strings.Contains(command, codexHookCommandInfix)
   266	}
   267	
   268	func codexHookCommand(event string) string {
   269		return yyorkHookExecutable() + " hooks codex " + event
   270	}
   271	
   272	func yyorkHookExecutable() string {
   273		executable, err := os.Executable()
   274		if err != nil || strings.TrimSpace(executable) == "" {
   275			return "yyork"
   276		}
   277		if strings.Contains(executable, "go-build") {
   278			cwd, err := os.Getwd()
   279			if err != nil || strings.TrimSpace(cwd) == "" {
   280				return "go run ./cmd/yyork"
   281			}
   282			if direnv, err := exec.LookPath("direnv"); err == nil {
   283				return shellQuote(direnv) + " exec " + shellQuote(cwd) + " go run ./cmd/yyork"
   284			}
   285			return "go run ./cmd/yyork"
   286		}
   287		return shellQuote(executable)
   288	}
   289	
   290	func shellQuote(value string) string {
   291		return "'" + strings.ReplaceAll(value, "'", "'\\''") + "'"
   292	}
   293	
   294	// removeCodexManagedHooks strips yyork hook entries from every group,
   295	// dropping any group left without hooks.
   296	func removeCodexManagedHooks(groups []codexMatcherGroup) []codexMatcherGroup {
   297		result := make([]codexMatcherGroup, 0, len(groups))
   298		for _, group := range groups {
   299			kept := make([]codexHookEntry, 0, len(group.Hooks))
   300			for _, hook := range group.Hooks {
   301				if !isCodexManagedHook(hook.Command) {
   302					kept = append(kept, hook)
   303				}
   304			}
   305			if len(kept) > 0 {
   306				group.Hooks = kept
   307				result = append(result, group)
   308			}
   309		}
   310		return result
   311	}
   312	
   313	func parseCodexHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, event string, target *[]codexMatcherGroup) error {
   314		data, ok := rawHooks[event]

   324	func marshalCodexHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, event string, groups []codexMatcherGroup) error {

   332		}

   334		return nil

   345		return false
   346	}

   349		if a == b {

   351		}

   358		}

    57	// runUninstallHooks removes the selected agent's yyork hooks from the

    59	func runUninstallHooks(ctx context.Context, agentName string, stdout io.Writer, stderr io.Writer) int {
    60		var manager hookManager
    61		switch agentName {

    63			manager = codex.New()
    64		case "claude-code":
    65			manager = claudecode.New()

    84			return 1

   102	
   103	// runAgentHook is the shared hook driver for every agent: it reads
   104	// YYORK_SESSION_ID, parses the payload, merges any normalized session
   105	// metadata into the AO store, and publishes session.updated. It always writes
   106	// the empty `{}` hook response and exits 0 when run outside an AO session or
   107	// when the row is missing, so a hook firing in a non-AO `claude`/`codex`
   108	// session is a harmless no-op. agentName is used only for diagnostics.
   109	func runAgentHook(ctx context.Context, agentName, event string, stdin io.Reader, stdout io.Writer, stderr io.Writer) int {
   110		if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
   111			fmt.Fprintf(stderr, "hooks %s %s: %v\n", agentName, event, err)
   112			return 1
   113		}
   114	
   115		aoSessionID := strings.TrimSpace(os.Getenv("YYORK_SESSION_ID"))
   116		if aoSessionID == "" {
   117			writeHookResponse(stdout)
   118			return 0
   119		}
   120	
   121		raw, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(stdin, 1<<20))
   122		if err != nil {
   123			fmt.Fprintf(stderr, "hooks %s %s: read payload: %v\n", agentName, event, err)
   124			return 1
   125		}
   126	
   127		dbPath, err := store.DefaultPath()
   128		if err != nil {
   129			fmt.Fprintf(stderr, "hooks %s %s: %v\n", agentName, event, err)
   130			return 1
   131		}
   132		dataStore, err := store.Open(ctx, dbPath)
   133		if err != nil {
   134			fmt.Fprintf(stderr, "hooks %s %s: open store: %v\n", agentName, event, err)
   135			return 1
   136		}
   137		defer func() { _ = dataStore.Close() }()
   138	
   139		repo := dataStore.Sessions()
   140		fields, err := hookFields(ctx, repo, aoSessionID, event, raw)
   141		if errors.Is(err, store.ErrSessionNotFound) {
   142			writeHookResponse(stdout)
   143			return 0
   144		}
   145		if err != nil {
   146			fmt.Fprintf(stderr, "hooks %s %s: %v\n", agentName, event, err)
   147			return 1
   148		}
   149	
   150		if len(fields) > 0 {
   151			if err := repo.MergeMetadata(ctx, aoSessionID, fields); errors.Is(err, store.ErrSessionNotFound) {
   152				writeHookResponse(stdout)
   153				return 0
   154			} else if err != nil {
   155				fmt.Fprintf(stderr, "hooks %s %s: merge metadata: %v\n", agentName, event, err)
   156				return 1
   157			}
   158			control.NewForwardingPublisher().Publish(events.NewSessionUpdated(aoSessionID))
   159		}
   160	
   161		writeHookResponse(stdout)
   162		return 0
   163	}
   164	
   165	// hookPayload projects the fields yyork reads from a hook's stdin JSON.
   166	// Codex and Claude Code expose these under identical names (session_id, prompt,
   167	// last_assistant_message — the latter verified present in Claude's Stop payload
   168	// despite older docs omitting it), so one shape serves both. Agent-specific
   169	// extras are ignored.
   170	type hookPayload struct {
   171		HookEventName        string  `json:"hook_event_name"`
   172		SessionID            string  `json:"session_id"`
   173		Prompt               string  `json:"prompt"`
   174		LastAssistantMessage *string `json:"last_assistant_message"`
   175	}
   176	
   177	// hookFields computes the normalized session-metadata fields to merge for one
   178	// hook event: agentSessionId at session start, title from the first user
   179	// prompt, and recap from the latest assistant message at stop. Returning
   180	// store.ErrSessionNotFound makes the driver no-op cleanly.
   181	func hookFields(ctx context.Context, repo store.SessionRepo, aoSessionID, event string, raw []byte) (map[string]any, error) {
   182		var payload hookPayload
   183		if err := unmarshalHookPayload(raw, &payload); err != nil {
   184			return nil, fmt.Errorf("decode payload: %w", err)
   185		}
   186	
   187		fields := map[string]any{}
   188		switch event {
   189		case "session-start":
   190			if sessionID := strings.TrimSpace(payload.SessionID); sessionID != "" {
   191				fields[hookMetadataAgentSessionID] = sessionID
   192			}
   193		case "user-prompt-submit":
   194			return titleFields(ctx, repo, aoSessionID, payload.Prompt)
   195		case "stop":
   196			if payload.LastAssistantMessage == nil {
   197				return fields, nil
   198			}
   199			if recap := compactHookText(*payload.LastAssistantMessage, hookRecapMaxLen); recap != "" {
   200				fields[hookMetadataRecap] = recap
   201			}
   202		default:
   203			return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown hook event %q", event)
   204		}
   205		return fields, nil
   206	}
   207	
   208	// titleFields sets the title from the user prompt, but only the first time (the
   209	// first user prompt titles the session; later prompts don't retitle it).
   210	func titleFields(ctx context.Context, repo store.SessionRepo, aoSessionID, prompt string) (map[string]any, error) {
   211		fields := map[string]any{}
   212		title := compactHookText(prompt, hookTitleMaxLen)
   213		if title == "" {
   214			return fields, nil
   215		}
   216		row, err := repo.Get(ctx, aoSessionID)
   217		if err != nil {
   218			return nil, err
   219		}
   220		if stringMetadata(row.Metadata, hookMetadataTitle) == "" {
   221			fields[hookMetadataTitle] = title
   222		}
   223		return fields, nil
   224	}
   225	
   226	// unmarshalHookPayload decodes a hook payload, tolerating an empty body (a hook
   227	// invoked with no stdin leaves the zero-valued payload).
   228	func unmarshalHookPayload(raw []byte, v any) error {
   229		if len(bytes.TrimSpace(raw)) == 0 {
   230			return nil
   231		}
   232		return json.Unmarshal(raw, v)
   233	}
   234	
   235	func compactHookText(text string, maxLen int) string {
   236		compact := strings.Join(strings.Fields(text), " ")
   237		if maxLen <= 0 || len(compact) <= maxLen {
   238			return compact
   239		}
   240		if maxLen <= 3 {
   241			return compact[:maxLen]
   242		}
   243		return strings.TrimSpace(compact[:maxLen-3]) + "..."
   244	}
   245	
   246	func stringMetadata(metadata map[string]any, key string) string {
   247		if value, ok := metadata[key].(string); ok {
   248			return value
   249		}
   250		return ""
   251	}
   252	
   253	func writeHookResponse(stdout io.Writer) {
   254		fmt.Fprintln(stdout, "{}")
   255	}

> AGENT

The install path confirms this is a prelaunch hook setup: yyork writes `.codex/hooks.json` during session spawn, and the Stop handler only updates `metadata.recap` when Codex later invokes that command with `YYORK_SESSION_ID` set.

> AGENT

You do **not** need a new Codex agent just to get a recap onto the existing card. That can be backfilled from the Codex transcript by writing the latest `last_agent_message` into `metadata.recap`, or by invoking `yyork hooks codex stop` manually with a `last_assistant_message` payload.

For **automatic recaps going forward**, yes, the clean path is a new Codex session **after fixing yyork first**. Spawning a new one with the current code can repeat the same bad hook command.

What needs to change:

1. Fix Codex hook generation in [internal/plugin/agent/codex/hooks.go](/Users/tanishqpalandurkar/Projects/yyork/internal/plugin/agent/codex/hooks.go:268). It currently derives the hook command from `os.Executable()`, which wrote `entire hooks codex stop` into the worktree. It needs to write a stable yyork command, e.g. the real `yyork` binary or a controlled env override.

2. Make `GetAgentHooks` replace bad managed hooks, not just skip “same suffix” duplicates. Right now `entire hooks codex stop` looks managed because it contains ` hooks codex `, so a reinstall may preserve the bad command.

3. Handle Codex hook trust for fresh worktrees. The hook file exists, but Codex may not trust project-local hooks unless yyork launches with hook trust bypass or pre-seeds trust.

4. Spawn a new Codex session after that. Hooks are installed before launch at [internal/session/engine.go](/Users/tanishqpalandurkar/Projects/yyork/internal/session/engine.go:270), so the reliable automatic path starts at spawn time.

For the currently visible `ygkp7s` card, the missing recap is recoverable without respawning. It just will not be retroactively produced by Codex because the Stop hook already failed.

> DEVELOPER

If I leave that requirement behind, the new codex agents spawned using yyork would hv the recap though right?

> AGENT

No, not with yyork exactly as it is right now.

A new Codex agent spawned by yyork would still go through the same hook install path, and that path is currently capable of writing the bad command:

```text
entire hooks codex stop
```

If that happens again, the new session still won’t get `metadata.recap`.

Once yyork is fixed to generate a real yyork hook command and handle Codex hook trust, then yes: newly spawned Codex agents should get recaps automatically on Stop. You would not need to backfill anything or manually intervene per session.

package codex

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"reflect"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/yyovil/yyork/internal/plugin/agent"
)

func TestGetLaunchCommandBuildsCrossPlatformArgv(t *testing.T) {
	plugin := &Plugin{resolvedBinary: "codex"}

	cmd, err := plugin.GetLaunchCommand(context.Background(), agent.LaunchConfig{
		Permissions:      agent.PermissionModeBypassPermissions,
		Prompt:           "-fix this",
		SystemPromptFile: filepath.Join("tmp", "prompt with spaces.md"),
		SystemPrompt:     "ignored",
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	want := []string{
		"codex",
		"-c", "check_for_update_on_startup=false",
		"--dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox",
		"-c", "model_instructions_file=" + filepath.Join("tmp", "prompt with spaces.md"),
		"--", "-fix this",
	}
	if !reflect.DeepEqual(cmd, want) {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected command\nwant: %#v\n got: %#v", want, cmd)
	}
}

func TestGetLaunchCommandMapsApprovalModes(t *testing.T) {
	tests := []struct {
		name        string
		permission  agent.PermissionMode
		want        []string
		notExpected string
	}{
		{
			name:        "default",
			permission:  agent.PermissionModeDefault,
			notExpected: "--ask-for-approval",
		},
		{
			name:       "accept-edits",
			permission: agent.PermissionModeAcceptEdits,
			want:       []string{"--ask-for-approval", "on-request"},
		},
		{
			name:       "auto",
			permission: agent.PermissionModeAuto,
			want:       []string{"--ask-for-approval", "on-request", "-c", `approvals_reviewer="auto_review"`},
		},
		{
			name:       "bypass-permissions",
			permission: agent.PermissionModeBypassPermissions,
			want:       []string{"--dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox"},
		},
		{
			name:        "empty",
			permission:  "",
			notExpected: "--ask-for-approval",
		},
	}

	for _, tt := range tests {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			plugin := &Plugin{resolvedBinary: "codex"}
			cmd, err := plugin.GetLaunchCommand(context.Background(), agent.LaunchConfig{
				Permissions: tt.permission,
			})
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatal(err)
			}
			if len(tt.want) > 0 && !containsSubsequence(cmd, tt.want) {
				t.Fatalf("command %#v does not contain %#v", cmd, tt.want)
			}
			if tt.notExpected != "" && contains(cmd, tt.notExpected) {
				t.Fatalf("command %#v contains %q", cmd, tt.notExpected)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestGetPromptDeliveryStrategyIsInCommand(t *testing.T) {
	plugin := &Plugin{resolvedBinary: "codex"}

	got, err := plugin.GetPromptDeliveryStrategy(context.Background(), agent.LaunchConfig{})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if got != agent.PromptDeliveryInCommand {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected strategy: %q", got)
	}
}

func TestGetConfigSpecHasNoCustomFieldsYet(t *testing.T) {
	plugin := &Plugin{resolvedBinary: "codex"}

	spec, err := plugin.GetConfigSpec(context.Background())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(spec.Fields) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("unexpected config fields: %#v", spec.Fields)
	}
}

func TestGetAgentHooksInstallsCodexHooks(t *testing.T) {
	plugin := &Plugin{resolvedBinary: "codex"}
	workspace := t.TempDir()
	hooksDir := filepath.Join(workspace, ".codex")
	if err := os.MkdirAll(hooksDir, 0o755); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	hooksPath := filepath.Join(hooksDir, "hooks.json")
	existing := `{"hooks":{"Stop":[{"matcher":null,"hooks":[{"type":"command","command":"custom stop hook","timeout":3}]}]}}`
	if err := os.WriteFile(hooksPath, []byte(existing), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	cfg := agent.WorkspaceHookConfig{
		DataDir:       t.TempDir(),
		SessionID:     "sess-1",
		WorkspacePath: workspace,
	}
	if err := plugin.GetAgentHooks(context.Background(), cfg); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	// A second install must not duplicate yyork hook commands.
	if err := plugin.GetAgentHooks(context.Background(), cfg); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	data, err := os.ReadFile(hooksPath)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var config codexHookFile
	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &config); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if config.Hooks == nil {
		t.Fatalf("hooks config missing hooks object: %#v", config)
	}
	for _, spec := range codexManagedHooks {
		entries := config.Hooks[spec.Event]
		if count := countCodexHookCommand(entries, spec.Command); count != 1 {
			t.Fatalf("%s command count = %d, want 1 in %#v", spec.Event, count, entries)
		}
	}
	stopEntries := config.Hooks["Stop"]
	if countCodexHookCommand(stopEntries, "custom stop hook") != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("existing Stop hook was not preserved: %#v", stopEntries)
	}

	configData, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(workspace, ".codex", "config.toml"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(string(configData), codexHooksFeatureLine) {
		t.Fatalf("config.toml missing hooks feature flag: %s", configData)
	}
}

func TestUninstallHooksRemovesCodexHooks(t *testing.T) {
	plugin := &Plugin{resolvedBinary: "codex"}
	workspace := t.TempDir()
	hooksPath := filepath.Join(workspace, ".codex", "hooks.json")

	ctx := context.Background()
	cfg := agent.WorkspaceHookConfig{DataDir: t.TempDir(), SessionID: "sess-1", WorkspacePath: workspace}

	// Pre-seed a user's own Stop hook; it must survive uninstall.
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(hooksPath), 0o755); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	existing := `{"hooks":{"Stop":[{"matcher":null,"hooks":[{"type":"command","command":"custom stop hook","timeout":3}]}]}}`
	if err := os.WriteFile(hooksPath, []byte(existing), 0o644); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	if err := plugin.GetAgentHooks(ctx, cfg); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if installed, err := plugin.AreHooksInstalled(ctx, workspace); err != nil || !installed {
		t.Fatalf("AreHooksInstalled after install = (%v, %v), want (true, nil)", installed, err)
	}

	if err := plugin.UninstallHooks(ctx, workspace); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if installed, err := plugin.AreHooksInstalled(ctx, workspace); err != nil || installed {
		t.Fatalf("AreHooksInstalled after uninstall = (%v, %v), want (false, nil)", installed, err)
	}

	data, err := os.ReadFile(hooksPath)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var config codexHookFile
	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &config); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, spec := range codexManagedHooks {
		if got := countCodexHookCommand(config.Hooks[spec.Event], spec.Command); got != 0 {
			t.Fatalf("%s command %q count = %d after uninstall, want 0", spec.Event, spec.Command, got)
		}
	}
	if countCodexHookCommand(config.Hooks["Stop"], "custom stop hook") != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("user Stop hook not preserved: %#v", config.Hooks["Stop"])
	}

	// The shared hooks feature flag in config.toml is left in place — it enables
	// every Codex hook, not just yyork's.
	configData, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(workspace, ".codex", "config.toml"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if !strings.Contains(string(configData), codexHooksFeatureLine) {
		t.Fatalf("config.toml hooks feature flag removed by uninstall: %s", configData)
	}
}

func TestSessionInfoReadsHookMetadata(t *testing.T) {
	plugin := &Plugin{resolvedBinary: "codex"}

	info, ok, err := plugin.SessionInfo(context.Background(), agent.SessionRef{
		WorkspacePath: "/some/path",
		Metadata: map[string]string{
			codexAgentSessionIDMetadataKey: "thread-123",
			codexTitleMetadataKey:          "Fix login redirect",
			codexRecapMetadataKey:          "Updated the auth callback and tests.",
			"ignored":                      "not returned",
		},
	})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v, want nil", err)
	}
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("ok = false, want true")
	}
	if info.AgentSessionID != "thread-123" {
		t.Fatalf("AgentSessionID = %q, want native id", info.AgentSessionID)
	}
	if info.Title != "Fix login redirect" {
		t.Fatalf("Title = %q, want hook title", info.Title)
	}
	if info.Recap != "Updated the auth callback and tests." {
		t.Fatalf("Recap = %q, want hook recap", info.Recap)
	}
	if info.Metadata != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Metadata = %#v, want nil for Codex", info.Metadata)
	}
}

func TestSessionInfoReadsLegacySummaryAsRecap(t *testing.T) {
	plugin := &Plugin{resolvedBinary: "codex"}

	info, ok, err := plugin.SessionInfo(context.Background(), agent.SessionRef{
		WorkspacePath: "/some/path",
		Metadata: map[string]string{
			codexLegacySummaryMetadataKey: "Legacy stop hook message.",
		},
	})
	if err != nil || !ok {
		t.Fatalf("SessionInfo = (ok=%v, err=%v), want ok", ok, err)

}

	}
}

func contains(values []string, needle string) bool {
	for _, value := range values {
		if value == needle {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

func containsSubsequence(values []string, needle []string) bool {
	if len(needle) == 0 {
		return true
	}

	for start := range values {
		if start+len(needle) > len(values) {
			return false
		}
		ok := true
		for offset, want := range needle {
			if values[start+offset] != want {
				ok = false
				break
			}
		}
		if ok {
			return true
		}
	}

	return false
}

func countCodexHookCommand(entries []codexMatcherGroup, command string) int {
	count := 0
	for _, entry := range entries {
		for _, hook := range entry.Hooks {
			if hook.Command == command {
				count++
			}
		}
	}
	return count
}

            while [ "$root" != "/" ] && [ ! -f "$root/pnpm-workspace.yaml" ]; do

            if [ ! -f "$root/pnpm-workspace.yaml" ]; then

#!/usr/bin/env node
import { spawn, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { createServer } from 'node:net';
import { dirname, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

import { resolveDevConfig, UsageError } from './yyork-config.mjs';

const rootDir = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const cliArgs = process.argv.slice(2);
if (cliArgs.length === 1 && (cliArgs[0] === '--help' || cliArgs[0] === '-h')) {
  printHelp();
  process.exit(0);
}
if (
  cliArgs.length === 1 &&
  (cliArgs[0] === '--version' || cliArgs[0] === '-v')
) {
  printVersion();
  process.exit(0);
}
if (cliArgs.length > 0) {
  if (cliArgs[0].startsWith('-')) {
    exitWithUsageError(`Unknown option: ${cliArgs[0]}`);
  }
  runBackendCLI(cliArgs);
}

const webDir = resolve(rootDir, 'web');
let devConfig;
try {
  devConfig = resolveDevConfig({ env: process.env, webDir });
} catch (error) {
  if (error instanceof UsageError) {
    exitWithUsageError(error.message);
  }

  throw error;
}
const { backendHost } = devConfig;
const backendPort = await findAvailablePort(devConfig.backendPort, backendHost);
const webPort = await findAvailableWebPort(devConfig.webPort);
const backendOrigin = `http://${backendHost}:${backendPort}`;
const webOrigin = `http://localhost:${webPort}`;

const backend = startBackend(backendHost, backendPort);
const web = spawn('pnpm', ['--dir', 'web', 'dev'], {
  cwd: rootDir,
  env: {
    ...process.env,
    VITE_BACKEND_ORIGIN: backendOrigin,
    VITE_PORT: String(webPort),
  },
  shell: process.platform === 'win32',
  stdio: 'inherit',
});

printStartupBanner({ backendOrigin, webOrigin });

let shuttingDown = false;
const children = [backend, web];

for (const signal of ['SIGINT', 'SIGTERM']) {
  process.on(signal, () => shutdown(signal));
}

for (const child of children) {
  child.on('exit', (code, signal) => {
    if (shuttingDown) {
      return;
    }
    shuttingDown = true;
    for (const other of children) {
      if (other !== child) {
        other.kill('SIGTERM');
      }
    }
    if (signal) {
      process.kill(process.pid, signal);
      return;
    }
    process.exit(code ?? 0);
  });
}

function startBackend(host, port) {
  const backendArgs = [
    'run',
    './cmd/yyork',
    '-addr',
    `${host}:${port}`,
    '-open=false',
  ];

  if (hasCommand('go', ['version'])) {
    return spawn('go', backendArgs, {
      cwd: rootDir,
      shell: process.platform === 'win32',
      stdio: 'inherit',
    });
  }

  if (process.platform !== 'win32' && hasCommand('nix', ['--version'])) {
    return spawn('nix', ['develop', '--command', 'go', ...backendArgs], {
      cwd: rootDir,
      stdio: 'inherit',
    });
  }

  console.error(
    'Unable to start the backend: install Go or run from a Nix dev shell.'
  );
  process.exit(1);
}

function runBackendCLI(args) {
  const backendArgs = ['run', './cmd/yyork', ...args];
  if (hasCommand('go', ['version'])) {
    const result = spawnSync('go', backendArgs, {
      cwd: rootDir,
      shell: process.platform === 'win32',
      stdio: 'inherit',
    });
    process.exit(result.status ?? 1);
  }

  const builtBackend = resolve(rootDir, 'yyork');
  if (existsSync(builtBackend)) {
    const result = spawnSync(builtBackend, args, {
      cwd: rootDir,
      stdio: 'inherit',
    });
    process.exit(result.status ?? 1);
  }

  if (process.platform !== 'win32' && hasCommand('nix', ['--version'])) {
    const result = spawnSync(
      'nix',
      ['develop', '--command', 'go', ...backendArgs],
      {
        cwd: rootDir,
        stdio: 'inherit',
      }
    );
    process.exit(result.status ?? 1);
  }

  console.error(
    'Unable to run the CLI: install Go or run from a Nix dev shell.'
  );
  process.exit(1);
}

function hasCommand(command, args) {
  const result = spawnSync(command, args, { stdio: 'ignore' });
  return result.status === 0;
}

// Vite-style highlighted banner for the two dev origins. Mirrors the Go
// backend's banner (internal/logging). Color is enabled on a TTY and
// suppressed when NO_COLOR is set or output is piped.
function printStartupBanner({ backendOrigin, webOrigin }) {
  const color = Boolean(process.stdout.isTTY) && !('NO_COLOR' in process.env);
  const paint = (codes, text) =>
    color ? `\u001b[${codes}m${text}\u001b[0m` : text;

  const badge = paint('1;30;48;5;212', ' yyork ');
  const arrow = paint('38;5;212', '➜');
  const label = (text) => paint('38;5;241', text.padEnd(7));
  const value = (text) => paint('4;38;5;86', text);

  process.stdout.write(`\n  ${badge}\n`);
  process.stdout.write(`  ${arrow}  ${label('web')}  ${value(webOrigin)}\n`);
  process.stdout.write(
    `  ${arrow}  ${label('backend')}  ${value(backendOrigin)}\n\n`
  );
}

func yyorkGoRunHookExecutable(root string) string {
	command := "cd " + shellQuote(root) + " && "
	if direnv, err := exec.LookPath("direnv"); err == nil {
		command += shellQuote(direnv) + " exec . "
	}
	return command + "go run ./cmd/yyork"
}

func shellQuote(value string) string {
	return "'" + strings.ReplaceAll(value, "'", "'\\''") + "'"
}

// removeCodexManagedHooks strips yyork hook entries from every group,
// dropping any group left without hooks.
func removeCodexManagedHooks(groups []codexMatcherGroup) []codexMatcherGroup {
	result := make([]codexMatcherGroup, 0, len(groups))
	for _, group := range groups {
		kept := make([]codexHookEntry, 0, len(group.Hooks))
		for _, hook := range group.Hooks {
			if !isCodexManagedHook(hook.Command) {
				kept = append(kept, hook)
			}
		}
		if len(kept) > 0 {
			group.Hooks = kept
			result = append(result, group)
		}
	}
	return result
}

func parseCodexHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, event string, target *[]codexMatcherGroup) error {
	data, ok := rawHooks[event]
	if !ok {
		return nil
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, target); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("parse %s hooks: %w", event, err)
	}
	return nil
}

func marshalCodexHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, event string, groups []codexMatcherGroup) error {
	if len(groups) == 0 {
		delete(rawHooks, event)
		return nil
	}
	data, err := json.Marshal(groups)
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("encode %s hooks: %w", event, err)
	}
	rawHooks[event] = data
	return nil
}

func codexHookCommandExists(groups []codexMatcherGroup, command string) bool {
	for _, group := range groups {
		for _, hook := range group.Hooks {

> AGENT

The first cleanup patch missed the exact local context. I’ve re-opened that section and am applying the removal with the current line shape.

> DEVELOPER

but this wont run in other people's computer though. would it?

> AGENT

You’re right to call that out. The hard-coded `/Users/...` command I wrote is only in the already-generated `ygkp7s` worktree hook file, not in the repo code. The repo code computes the local source root at hook-install time, so another machine would get its own path, but that still makes generated hook files machine-local artifacts.

For the product behavior, I think the better default is to generate `yyork hooks codex ...` when yyork is in `PATH`, and only use `cd <local repo> && go run ./cmd/yyork ...` as a dev fallback. I’m going to adjust that so other machines don’t get source-root commands unless they’re actually running from an uninstalled dev checkout.

> AGENT

The portable variant is to rely on Codex running hooks from the session workspace, which is already the yyork worktree. In that context `go run ./cmd/yyork hooks codex ...` is both machine-independent and avoids the noisy `yyork` dev wrapper. I’m checking the launch path once, then I’ll switch the generated command to that.

// Package codex implements the Codex agent plugin: launching new sessions,
// resuming hook-tracked sessions, installing workspace-local hooks, and reading
// hook-derived session info.
//
// yyork-managed sessions derive native session identity and display
// metadata from Codex hooks instead of transcript/cache scans.
package codex

import (
	"context"
	"os"
	"os/exec"
	"path/filepath"
	"runtime"
	"strings"
	"sync"

	"github.com/yyovil/yyork/internal/plugin"
	"github.com/yyovil/yyork/internal/plugin/agent"
	"github.com/yyovil/yyork/internal/utils"
)

const (
	codexAgentSessionIDMetadataKey = "agentSessionId"
	codexTitleMetadataKey          = "title"
	codexRecapMetadataKey          = "recap"
	codexLegacySummaryMetadataKey  = "summary"
)

type Plugin struct {
	binaryMu       sync.Mutex
	resolvedBinary string
}

func New() *Plugin {
	return &Plugin{}
}

var _ plugin.Plugin = (*Plugin)(nil)
var _ agent.Agent = (*Plugin)(nil)

func (p *Plugin) Manifest() plugin.Manifest {
	return plugin.Manifest{
		ID:          "codex",
		Name:        "Codex",
		Description: "Run Codex worker sessions.",
		Version:     "0.0.1",
		Capabilities: []plugin.Capability{
			plugin.CapabilityAgent,
		},
	}
}

func (p *Plugin) GetConfigSpec(ctx context.Context) (agent.ConfigSpec, error) {
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return agent.ConfigSpec{}, err
	}
	return agent.ConfigSpec{}, nil
}

func (p *Plugin) GetLaunchCommand(ctx context.Context, cfg agent.LaunchConfig) (cmd []string, err error) {
	binary, err := p.codexBinary(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	cmd = []string{binary}
	appendNoUpdateCheckFlag(&cmd)
	appendApprovalFlags(&cmd, cfg.Permissions)

	if cfg.SystemPromptFile != "" {
		cmd = append(cmd, "-c", "model_instructions_file="+cfg.SystemPromptFile)
	} else if cfg.SystemPrompt != "" {
		cmd = append(cmd, "-c", "developer_instructions="+cfg.SystemPrompt)
	}

	if cfg.Prompt != "" {
		cmd = append(cmd, "--", cfg.Prompt)
	}

	return cmd, nil
}

func (p *Plugin) GetPromptDeliveryStrategy(ctx context.Context, cfg agent.LaunchConfig) (agent.PromptDeliveryStrategy, error) {
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return agent.PromptDeliveryInCommand, nil
}

// GetRestoreCommand rebuilds the argv that continues an existing Codex
// session: `codex resume <agentSessionId>`. ok is false when the hook-derived
// native session id has not landed yet, so callers can fall back to fresh
// launch behavior.
func (p *Plugin) GetRestoreCommand(ctx context.Context, cfg agent.RestoreConfig) (cmd []string, ok bool, err error) {
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return nil, false, err
	}
	agentSessionID := strings.TrimSpace(cfg.Session.Metadata[codexAgentSessionIDMetadataKey])
	if agentSessionID == "" {
		return nil, false, nil
	}

	binary, err := p.codexBinary(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, false, err
	}

	cmd = []string{binary, "resume"}
	appendNoUpdateCheckFlag(&cmd)
	appendApprovalFlags(&cmd, cfg.Permissions)
	cmd = append(cmd, agentSessionID)
	return cmd, true, nil
}

// SessionInfo surfaces Codex hook-derived metadata. Metadata is intentionally
// nil for Codex: callers get the normalized fields directly.
func (p *Plugin) SessionInfo(ctx context.Context, session agent.SessionRef) (agent.SessionInfo, bool, error) {
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return agent.SessionInfo{}, false, err
	}
	info := agent.SessionInfo{
		AgentSessionID: session.Metadata[codexAgentSessionIDMetadataKey],
		Title:          session.Metadata[codexTitleMetadataKey],
		Recap:          metadataValue(session.Metadata, codexRecapMetadataKey, codexLegacySummaryMetadataKey),
	}
	if info.AgentSessionID == "" && info.Title == "" && info.Recap == "" {
		return agent.SessionInfo{}, false, nil
	}
	return info, true, nil
}

func metadataValue(metadata map[string]string, keys ...string) string {
	for _, key := range keys {
		if value := strings.TrimSpace(metadata[key]); value != "" {
			return value
		}
	}
	return ""
}

// ResolveCodexBinary returns the path to the codex binary on this machine,
// searching PATH then a handful of well-known install locations
// (Homebrew, Cargo, npm global). Returns "codex" as a last-ditch fallback
// so callers see a clear "command not found" rather than an empty argv.
func ResolveCodexBinary(ctx context.Context) (string, error) {
	if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
		return "", err
	}

	if runtime.GOOS == "windows" {
		for _, name := range []string{"codex.cmd", "codex.exe", "codex"} {
			path, err := exec.LookPath(name)
			if err == nil && path != "" {
				return path, nil
			}
			if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
				return "", err
			}
		}

		candidates := []string{}
		if appData := os.Getenv("APPDATA"); appData != "" {
			candidates = append(candidates,
				filepath.Join(appData, "npm", "codex.cmd"),
				filepath.Join(appData, "npm", "codex.exe"),
			)
		}
		if home, err := os.UserHomeDir(); err == nil {
			candidates = append(candidates, filepath.Join(home, ".cargo", "bin", "codex.exe"))
		}
		for _, candidate := range candidates {
			if utils.FileExists(candidate) {
				return candidate, nil
			}
			if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
				return "", err
			}
		}

		return "codex", nil
	}

	if path, err := exec.LookPath("codex"); err == nil && path != "" {
		return path, nil
	}

	candidates := []string{
		"/usr/local/bin/codex",
		"/opt/homebrew/bin/codex",
	}
	if home, err := os.UserHomeDir(); err == nil {
		candidates = append(candidates,
			filepath.Join(home, ".cargo", "bin", "codex"),
			filepath.Join(home, ".npm", "bin", "codex"),
		)
	}

	for _, candidate := range candidates {
		if utils.FileExists(candidate) {
			return candidate, nil
		}
		if err := ctx.Err(); err != nil {
			return "", err
		}
	}

	return "codex", nil
}

func (p *Plugin) codexBinary(ctx context.Context) (string, error) {
	p.binaryMu.Lock()
	defer p.binaryMu.Unlock()

	if p.resolvedBinary != "" {
		return p.resolvedBinary, nil
	}

	binary, err := ResolveCodexBinary(ctx)
	if err != nil {

> AGENT

The durability layer does set the zellij pane cwd to the session worktree, so the relative `go run ./cmd/yyork` command is valid for yyork-created worktrees. I’m changing both the code and the already-generated `ygkp7s` hook file to that portable command.

> AGENT

The first pass left the source-root value only as a guard, so I’m tightening that now to avoid an unused-value issue and keep the helper intentionally relative.

> AGENT

To fully answer the portability concern, I’m also avoiding an absolute `direnv` path. The generated dev command should be worktree-relative: `direnv exec . go run ./cmd/yyork ...`, or `go run ./cmd/yyork ...` if `direnv` is not available.

> AGENT

I’m cleaning that small Go shape so the code reads naturally: the lookup is just a presence check now.

> AGENT

The live hook file is now machine-independent. I’m verifying the relative command from inside the `ygkp7s` worktree, which is the same cwd Codex/Zellij uses.

> AGENT

That exposed a real issue: `direnv exec .` is not suitable inside fresh worktrees because the copied `.envrc` can be blocked. I’m dropping `direnv` from the generated hook command and using plain `go run ./cmd/yyork`, which works from yyork-created repo worktrees without per-worktree direnv approval.

> AGENT

You were right. The first live-file patch was machine-local, so I changed it again.

Now the repo code generates a portable dev hook command:

```text
go run ./cmd/yyork hooks codex stop
```

That works because yyork-created Zellij sessions run from the session worktree. I also changed the existing `ygkp7s` hook file to use that same relative command, so it no longer has `/Users/...`, `/nix/store/...`, or `direnv`.

Code changes:
- [hooks.go](/Users/tanishqpalandurkar/Projects/yyork/internal/plugin/agent/codex/hooks.go:84) now removes stale yyork-managed Codex hooks before adding the current one, so `entire hooks codex ...` gets replaced.
- [hooks.go](/Users/tanishqpalandurkar/Projects/yyork/internal/plugin/agent/codex/hooks.go:272) no longer trusts arbitrary `os.Executable()` values like `entire`.
- [codex_test.go](/Users/tanishqpalandurkar/Projects/yyork/internal/plugin/agent/codex/codex_test.go:172) adds regression coverage for replacing stale `entire hooks codex ...` commands.

Verified:
- From `ygkp7s`, `YYORK_SESSION_ID=__missing__ go run ./cmd/yyork hooks codex stop` returns `{}`.
- `go test ./internal/plugin/agent/codex ./cmd/yyork` passes.
- `ygkp7s` metadata was not modified or backfilled.

One operational note: the already-running yyork backend still has old code in memory. Restart yyork before testing a newly spawned Codex agent through the UI. The current `ygkp7s` hook file itself is repaired.

Unrelated dirty files still present and untouched: `README.md`, `canvas-web-preview.tsx`, and `session-workspace.ts`.

> DEVELOPER

alright tell me what's the expected behaviour of the newly spawned codex agent through yyork now?

> AGENT

I’ll frame this as the product behavior after the yyork backend is restarted, since newly spawned sessions depend on the in-memory backend using the patched hook generator.

> AGENT

Expected behavior for a newly spawned Codex agent through yyork now:

1. yyork creates the worktree and writes `.codex/hooks.json` before Codex starts.

2. The Codex hook file should contain portable commands like:

```text
go run ./cmd/yyork hooks codex stop
```

not `entire hooks codex stop`.

3. Codex starts in the session worktree, so that relative command resolves against the checked-out yyork repo.

4. When Codex emits hook events:
   - `SessionStart` stores Codex’s native session id as `metadata.agentSessionId`.
   - `UserPromptSubmit` can set the first prompt-derived title.
   - `Stop` sends `last_assistant_message`; yyork compacts it and writes `metadata.recap`.

5. yyork publishes `session.updated`, so the sidebar/Kanban card should update with the recap after the Codex turn completes.

Two caveats:
- Restart the yyork backend first. The currently running backend still has the old hook-generation code.
- This fixes the bad command. It does not yet solve any separate Codex hook-trust prompt/blocking behavior. If Codex refuses project-local hooks until trusted, the hook still may not fire automatically until that is handled.
